QUESTION BANK 2 - LOW-LATENCY/HFT PUBLIC ISSUE EVIDENCE INDEX
Compiled 2026-10-23. No coding solutions.
GitHub source discovery:
https://github.com/search?q=low+latency&type=repositories&p=2
https://github.com/search?q=hft&type=repositories

EVIDENCE RULES
Each numbered entry links to an actual public GitHub issue discovered with
the connected account. ISSUE LEAD does not mean confirmed bug, fixed bug,
or evidence of a particular root cause. An issue may be a user question,
false positive, or enhancement request. Only its indexed title/link is
collected in bulk; full discussions and fixes have NOT all been inspected.
Do not call the final count a count of confirmed failures.
Existing question bank1.txt is not modified. Entries already cited by
exact URL there are excluded from this collection when possible.
An unsolved first task appears with every case. NO SOLUTIONS or patches.

SOURCE-INSPECTION ORDER
Read full issue body; find failing input and actual observed outcome;
check replies and exact source commit; find reproduction/regression test;
compare alternative explanations; only then assign verdict.
If original input missing, record 'NO REPRODUCER'.

PACKT
The MIT-licensed Packt upstream source is linked as a pinned git submodule
at third_party/Building-Low-Latency-Applications-with-CPP.
40 inspected source-level questions appear separately in 'faillure predictions'.
The linked submodule is NOT a self-contained vendored archive.
